This serves as the base walker for traversing the pipeline graph.\\n\\nThe TransformHierarchy implements the visitor pattern with its nodes maintaining parent-child relationships and supporting both composite and primitive transforms. For example, the SplittableParDoViaKeyedWorkItems class (runners/core-java/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/core/SplittableParDoViaKeyedWorkItems.java) uses this mechanism to override splittable ParDo implementations with keyed work item processing.\\n\\n#### 3. **ExecutableStagePayload Construction**\\n\\nThe ExecutableStage interface and related classes handle the construction of executable stages:\\n- **ExecutableStage** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/util/construction/graph/ExecutableStage.java): A combination of PTransforms that can be executed within a single SDK harness. invocations.\\n- **FusedPipeline** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/util/construction/graph/FusedPipeline.java): Represents a pipeline after fusion into executable stages.\\n- **QueryablePipeline** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/util/construction/graph/QueryablePipeline.java): Provides a queryable interface over pipeline components.\\n- format.\\n\\n#### 5. **BoundedSource/UnboundedSource Adapter Classes**\\n\\nThese adapter classes enable flexible source handling:\\n- **BoundedToUnboundedSourceAdapter** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/util/construction/UnboundedReadFromBoundedSource.java): Wraps a BoundedSource as an UnboundedSource, enabling bounded sources to be used in streaming contexts.\\n- **BoundedReadFromUnboundedSource** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/io/BoundedReadFromUnboundedSource.java): Wraps an UnboundedSource with specified bounds on records (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/util/construction/UnboundedReadFromBoundedSource.java): Wraps a BoundedSource as an UnboundedSource, enabling bounded sources to be used in streaming contexts.\\n- **BoundedReadFromUnboundedSource** (sdks/java/core/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/sdk/io/BoundedReadFromUnboundedSource.java): Wraps an UnboundedSource with specified bounds on records and read time to create a BoundedSource.\\n- **BoundedSourceSystem** (runners/samza/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/samza/adapter/BoundedSourceSystem.java): Samza runner adapter for bounded sources, providing SystemFactory integration.\\n- **UnboundedSourceSystem** (runners/samza/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/samza/adapter/UnboundedSourceSystem.java): Samza runner adapter for unbounded sources with checkpoint support.\\n- **MicrobatchSource** (runners/spark/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/spark/io/MicrobatchSource.java): Spark runner wrapper for UnboundedSource that accommodates Spark's micro-batch oriented nature.\\n\\n#### 6. **Core Runner Internals**\\n\\nThe runners/core-java package contains essential runner implementation utilities:\\n- **StateInternals** (runners/core-java/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/core/StateInternals.java): Interface describing state functionality a runner must provide.\\n- **TimerInternals** (runners/core-java/src/main/java/org/apache/beam/runners/core/TimerInternals.java): Interface for timer management in runners.\\n- **StateInternalsFactory** and **TimerInternalsFactory** (in runners/core-java): Provide key-scoped state and timer internals for keyed operations.\\n\\nThese components work together to enable runners to translate a user-defined Beam pipeline into runner-specific execution primitives while maintaining the semantic correctness of the pipeline computation.\\n\"\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
